    SELECT s."Id", s."EventStaffId", s."QualificationId", s."QualificationName", s0."Id" AS "Id0", s0."LicenseActiveDate", s0."LicenseExpiryDate", s0."LicenseNumber", s0."LicenseState", s0."LicenseType", s0."StaffQualificationId", s1."Id" AS "Id1", s1."Attribute", s1."StaffQualificationId" AS "StaffQualificationId0"
